39b3(gzungs 'dus): bgegs rnams rnam par 'joms//thob mdzad dpe med gsung ba po//rdo rje 'jig rten gcig snang ba//skyes pa bud med tshogs kyi bdag//nges par yon tan 'byor mdzad pa/
39b4(gzungs 'dus): sems can 'khor bar zhi bar mdzad//brtan po sdig pa rnams dang bral//kun du rnam rgyal snang ba po//kun du rgyas pa'i dkyil 'khor can//kun du rgya mtsho'i snang
39b5(gzungs 'dus): ba mdzad//kun du nges pa sel ba lags//mi bzang nga sa 'og rnam par bkrugs//de bzhin bzang mdzad thug par mdzad//nyon mongs rgya mtsho rnam par dkrugs nyon
39b6(gzungs 'dus): mongs phyogs rnams zad par mdzad//'bar ba'i dkar ba 'phro mdzad pa//rgyags shing bde ba zhi bar mdzad//rdo rje chen po stobs bskyed pa//snang ba thams cad nges par 'joms//
39b7(gzungs 'dus): pha rol bzhugs pa'i sdig sel//dag pa nam mkha'i spyod yul can 'bras bu sbyin zhing 'bras bu sbyin bzhing kun sbyin pa//ye shes sbyin zhing bsod nams sbyin//longs spyon zhing rgyal srid sbyin
39b8(gzungs 'dus): dbang phyug sbyin zhing tshig sbyin la//nor sbyin tshogs ldan rdo rje can//gzugs sbyin dpa' bo rnam par rgyal//dga' cing dge ba'i tshul sbyin pa//bde 'byung btsug na nor bu thogs
40a1(gzungs 'dus): @#//sa 'dzin bzung par dga' ba po//dkyil 'khor mgu bar sgrub mdzad pa//'bad cing de bzhin 'bebs par mdzad//rin chen dra ba rin chen mnyam//sgrol ma mun sel
40a2(gzungs 'dus): rgyal ba can //yon tan ye shes theg chen ldan //brten mchog che ba'i bdag nyid can//gsung ba chen po ye shes che//sangs rgyas tshul ni rab ston pa//mchod pa chen po theg chen gyi//
40a3(gzungs 'dus): theg pa bla na med par ldan//mchod pa chen po chos che ba//'bras bu chen po'i chos kyi mchod//ston mdzad 'dul mdzad ston pa po//zhi ba de bzhin zhi mdzad pa//bzang po bzang
40a4(gzungs 'dus): dang mtshungs pa nyid //kun du rab 'jam sel mdzad pa//snying rje ye shes snying po mkhyen//snying rje'i 'od kyis dkyil 'khor can //snying rje chen po yongs su rdzogs//

40a6(gzungs 'dus): can //kun du spyod pa'i khong dge ba//dge ba dam pa sbyin pa po//kun du yul kun mnga' ba ste//zhes bzang ba rtag tu mnga'//dul ba sangs rgyas kun dang mnyam //dkyil 'khor
